## Kebijakan Pelatihan Dewan Komisaris

Perseroan memberikan kesempatan kepada seluruh anggota  Dewan  Komisaris  untuk  mengikuti  pelatihan  dan pengembangan  kompetensi  terkait  dengan  peningkatan kapabilitas  dan  kompentensinya.  Sepanjang  tahun  2022, pengembangan dilakukan dalam berbagai program pelatihan internal dan eksternal, serta lokakarya dan seminar.

## Penilaian terhadap Kinerja Dewan Komisaris

Penilaian  terhadap  kinerja  Dewan  Komisaris  dan  masingmasing anggota Dewan Komisaris dilakukan secara rutin  setiap  tahun  melalui  penilaian  secara  mandiri  ( selfassessment ). Proses penilaian tersebut dilaksanakan melalui kriteria berikut:

1. Penilaian kinerja individu masing-masing anggota Dewan Komisaris;
2. Evaluasi kompetensi dan keahlian/pengalaman;
3. Efektivitas pelaksanaan tanggung jawab dan proses kerja Dewan Komisaris.

<!-- image -->

## Training Policy of the Board of Commissioners

The  Company  provides  opportunities  for  all  members  of the  Board  of  Commissioners  to  participate  in  training  and competency development with the aim of improving their capabilities  and  competencies.  In  2022,  the  training  and competency  development  was  carried  out  through  both internal  and  external  training  programs,  workshops,  and seminars.

## Assessment of the Board of Commissioners' Performance

Performance  appraisal  of  the  Board  of  Commissioners, both individually and collectively, is carried out using selfassessment procedures on an annual basis. The assessment is carried out through several criteria as follows:

1. Individual performance appraisal of each member of the Board of Commissioners;
2. Evaluation of the competency and expertise/experience;
3. The  effectiveness  of  the  implementation  of  duties  and responsibilities of the Board of Commissioners.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Penilaian dilakukan sesuai dengan dengan peraturan perundangan-undangan dan/atau Anggaran Dasar Perseroan. Selanjutnya, hasil penilaian tersebut akan disampaikan ke dalam Laporan Tahunan Perseroan sebagai bentuk pertanggungjawaban pelaksanaan tugas dan tanggung jawab pada saat RUPST.

The assesment shall be carried out in accordance with the applicable  laws  and  regulations  and/or  the  Company's Articles of Association. Thereafter, the results of the evaluation  shall  be  submitted  in  the  Annual  Report  as  a form  of  accountability  for  implementing  the  duties  and responsibilities, to be presented at the AGMS.

## Penilaian Kinerja Komite di Bawah Dewan Komisaris

Dalam melaksanakan tugas pengawasan, Dewan Komisaris membentuk Komite yang bertanggung jawab kepada Dewan Komisaris.  Saat  ini  Dewan  Komisaris  memiliki  satu  komite dibawah  Dewan  Komisaris  yaitu Komite  Audit. Kinerja Komite  Audit  dievaluasi  setiap  1  (satu)  tahun  sekali  oleh Dewan Komisaris. Dewan Komisaris melakukan pengawasan terhadap aspek laporan kondisi keuangan, manajemen risiko, nominasi  dan  remunerasi,  kepatuhan  terhadap  peraturan perundang-undangan,  kasus  hukum  yang  signifikan  (jika ada) serta audit eksternal sehingga kinerja Perseroan sepanjang tahun ini tetap berjalan dengan baik.

Kriteria umum  yang  menjadi  standar penilaian adalah ketepatan waktu penyampaian laporan, kualitas dan akurasi laporan, tingkat kehadiran rapat serta pencapaian KPI.

Penilaian  kinerja  tersebut  dilakukan  baik  secara  individual maupun  kolektif  setiap  tahunnya  dengan  metode selfassessment .    Hasil  penilaian  ini  tercermin  kepada  laporan pelaksanaan tugas serta tanggung jawab yang disampaikan oleh Komite.

2

0

## Assessment  of  the  Committees  under  the  Board  of Commissioners

In  order  to  carry  out  its  supervisory  duties,  the  Board  of Commissioners  forms  a  committee  that  is  responsible  for supporting  the  Board  of  Commissioners.  Currently,  the Board of Commissioners has 1 (one) committee under the Board of Commissioners, namely the Audit Committee. The performance  of  the  Audit  Committee  shall  be  evaluated annually  by  the  Board  of  Commissioners.  The  Board  of Commissioners oversees the financial statements, risk management,  nomination  and  remuneration,  compliance with the applicable laws and regulations, significant legal cases (if any), and external audits to ensure that the Company's operations are running well throughout the year.

In  general,  the  main  criteria  of  assessment  consist  of  the timeliness  of  submission  of  the  report,  report  quality  and accuracy,  the  attendance  rate  at  the  meetings,  and  the achievement of Key Performance Indicators (KPIs).

On  an  annual  basis,  the  performance  appraisal  is  carried out both individually and collectively using self-assessment procedures. The results of the performance appraisal will be summarized in the report on the implementation of duties and responsibilities submitted by the Committee.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Direksi  merupakan  organ  Perseroan  yang  berwenang  dan bertanggung  jawab  penuh  atas  kegiatan  dan  pengurusan Perseroan untuk mencapai kepentingan dan tujuan Perseroan sesuai dengan ketentuan Anggaran Dasar Perseroan.

Dalam struktur GCG Perseroan, Direksi bertanggung jawab kepada pemegang saham melalui RUPS dengan pengawasan yang dilaksanakan oleh Dewan Komisaris.

## Pengangkatan dan Pemberhentian Direksi

Komposisi  Direksi  Perseroan  paling  kurang  terdiri  dari  3 (tiga) orang anggota, dengan 1 (satu) di antaranya diangkat menjadi  Direktur  Utama.  Anggota  Direksi  diangkat  oleh RUPS, masing-masing untuk jangka waktu terhitung sejak  pengangkatannya  sampai  penutupan  RUPST  ketiga berikutnya,  dengan  tidak  mengurangi  hak  RUPS  untuk memberhentikannya sewaktu-waktu.

Jabatan anggota Direksi dapat berakhir apabila:

- a. Mengundurkan diri;
- b. Tidak  lagi  memenuhi  persyaratan  peraturan  OJK  dan peraturan perundang-undangan lainnya yang berlaku;
- c. Meninggal dunia;
- d. Masa jabatannya berakhir dan tidak diangkat lagi;
- e. Dinyatakan  pailit  atau  ditaruh  di  bawah  pengampuan berdasarkan surat keputusan pengadilan; dan
- f. Diberhentikan berdasarkan keputusan RUPS.

## Pedoman dan Kode Etik Direksi

Direksi Perseroan melaksanakan tugasnya berlandaskan pada piagam yang menjadi pedoman bagi setiap anggota Direksi. Pedoman  tersebut  mengatur  mengenai  landasan  hukum; tugas,  tanggung  jawab,  dan  wewenang;  nilai-nilai;  waktu kerja; kebijakan rapat, termasuk kebijakan kehadiran dalam rapat dan risalah rapat; pelaporan dan pertanggungjawaban serta benturan kepentingan.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

The Board of Directors is the Company's organ who holds full authority and responsibility for the activities and management of the Company to achieve the interests and objectives of the Company in accordance with the provisions of the Company's Articles of Association.

In  the  Company's GCG structure, the Board of Directors is responsible  to  the  shareholders  through  GMS  within  the Board of Commissioners' supervision.

## Appointment and Dismissal Board of Directors

The composition of the Board of Directors of the Company consists of at least 3 (three) members, of which 1 (one) member  is  appointed  as  the  President  Director.  Members of the Board of Directors are appointed by the GMS, for a period commencing from their appointment until the closing of the following third AGM, without prejudice to the right of the GMS to dismiss them at any time.

The position of a member of the Board of Directors may end if:

- a.

Resign;

- b. No longer meets the requirements of the FSA regulations and other applicable laws and regulations;
- c. Passed away;
- d. Term of service expires and is not reappointed;
- e. Had  been  declared  bankrupt  or  placed  under  custody based on a court decision; and
- f. Dismissed based on the GMS resolution.

## The  Guidelines  and  Code  of  Conduct  of  Board  of Directors' Charter

The  Board  of  Directors  of  the  Company  conduct  their duties  based  on  Charter  for  the  Board  of  Directors.  The Charter  stipulates  the  following  matters,  namely  the  legal basis; duties, responsibilities, and authorities; values; working  period; meeting  policies, including  attendance in  the  meetings  and  minutes  of  meetings;  reporting  and accountability; and conflict of interest.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Tugas, Wewenang, dan Tanggung Jawab Direksi

<!-- image -->

## Duties, Authorities, and Responsibilities of the Board of Directors

Berdasarkan Anggaran Dasar, Direksi memiliki tugas, wewenang, dan tanggung jawab sebagai berikut:

1. Menjalankan  dan  bertanggung  jawab  atas  pengurusan Perseroan untuk kepentingan Perseroan sesuai dengan maksud  dan  tujuan  Perseroan  yang  ditetapkan  dalam Anggaran Dasar Perseroan.
2. Wajib menyelenggarakan RUPST dan RUPSLB sebagaimana diatur dalam peraturan perundangundangan dan Anggaran Dasar Perseroan.
3. Mewakili  Perseroan  di  dalam  dan  di  luar  pengadilan tentang segala hal dan dalam segala kejadian, mengikat Perseroan  dengan  pihak  lain  dan  pihak  lain  dengan Perseroan, serta menjalankan segala tindakan, baik yang mengenai kepengurusan maupun  kepemilikan, akan tetapi dengan berbagai pembatasan tertentu.
4. Direktur Utama berhak dan berwenang dalam bertindak untuk dan atas nama Direksi serta mewakili Perseroan.

Segala tindakan dari para anggota Direksi yang bertentangan dengan Anggaran Dasar adalah tidak sah. Pembagian tugas dan wewenang setiap anggota Direksi ditetapkan oleh RUPS dan  wewenang  tersebut  oleh  RUPS  dapat  dilimpahkan kepada Dewan Komisaris, dalam hal RUPS tidak menetapkan maka  pembagian  tugas  dan  wewenang  anggota  Direksi ditetapkan berdasarkan keputusan Direksi.

Pembagian tugas dan tanggung jawab Direksi berdasarkan keputusan  Direksi  dengan  memperhatikan  keahlian  dan pengalaman masing-masing Direksi adalah sebagai berikut:

Based on the Articles of Association, the Board of Directors has the following duties, authorities, and responsibilities as the following :

1. To manage  the Company  and be responsible for the  management  in  the  interests  of  the  Company in  accordance  to  the  objectives  of  the  Company  as stipulated in the Company's Articles of Association.
2. To  conduct  AGM  and  EGM  as  stipulated  in  laws  and regulations and the Company's Articles of Association.
3. The President Director has the right and authority to act for and on behalf of the Board of Directors and represent the  Company,  as  well  as  carrying  out  all  actions,  both regarding management and ownership, but with certain restrictions.
4. President Director has the rights and authorities to act for and on behalf of the Board of Directors as well as to represent the Company.

Any action by the members of the Board of Directors that contradic to the Articles of Association is invalid. The division of  duties  and  authorities  of  each  member of the Board of Directors is determined by the GMS and the authority by the GMS may be conferred to the Board of Commissioners, in the event that the GMS does not determine the distribution of  duties  and  authorities  of  the  members  of  the  Board  of Directors, those shall be determined based on the resolution of the Board of Directors.

The division of the Board of Directors' duties and responsibilities according to the Board of Directors' rulings by  taking  into  account  the  skills  and  experience  of  each Director are as follows:

## Training of the Board of Directors

Untuk menunjang pelaksanaan tugasnya, Direksi mengikuti program pelatihan dalam rangka meningkatkan kompetensi yang dilaksanakan baik secara formal maupun  secara informal.  Para  anggota Direksi akan berbagi informasi dan pengetahuan  ( sharing  knowledge ) dari pendidikan dan pelatihan  yang  telah  diikutinya  kepada  sesama  anggota Direksi. Di tahun 2022, anggota Direksi mengikuti  seminar Strengthening  Economic  Growth  in  Dynamic  Conditions yang diselenggarakan oleh BEI pada 24 November 2022.

In an effort to support the implementation of its duties, the Board of Directors participates in both formal and informal training programs with the aim of improving its competency. Afterward, members of the Board of Directors will conduct information  and  knowledge  sharing  on  the  training  to other  respective  members  of  the  Board  of  Directors.  In 2022, the Board of Directors has participated in, seminar of Strengthening Economic Growth in Dynamic Conditions held by IDX on 24 th  November 2022.

## Penilaian terhadap Kinerja Direksi

Penilaian terhadap kinerja Direksi dan masing-masing anggota Direksi dilakukan secara rutin setiap tahun melalui penilaian secara mandiri (self-assessment) . Penilaian ini dilakukan secara individual dan kolegial dengan menggunakan  metode Key  Performance  Indicators (KPI). Proses penilaian tersebut mencakup kriteria sebagai berikut:

1. Penilaian  kinerja  individu  masing  -  masing  anggota Direksi;
2. Evaluasi kompetensi dan keahlian/pengalaman; dan
3. Efektivitas pelaksanaan tanggung jawab dan proses kerja Direksi.

KPI yang digunakan saat penilaian terhadap kinerja Direksi mencakup hal berikut:

1. Kinerja Keuangan;
2. Kepuasan Pelanggan;
3. Tata Kelola dan Kepatuhan; dan
4. Pengelolaan Sumber Daya Manusia.

Hasil penilaian tersebut akan disimpulkan ke dalam laporan dan  diajukan  kepada  Dewan  Komisaris,  untuk  kemudian disampaikan kepada pemegang saham melalui RUPS.

## Penilaian Komite-Komite di Bawah Direksi

Saat ini, Perseroan belum memiliki komite di bawah Direksi, sehingga  laporan  ini  belum  dapat  menyajikan  informasi terkait komite di bawah Direksi.

2

0

## Assessment of the Board of Directors' Performance

Performance  appraisal  of  the  Board  of  Directors,  both individually and collectively, is carried out using selfassessment procedures on an annual basis. This evaluation is  carried  out  individually  and  collectively  using  the  Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) method. The main criteria for performance appraisal consist of:

1. Individual performance appraisal of  each member of the Board of Directors;
2. Evaluation of competency and expertise/experience; and
3. The  effectiveness  of  the  implementation  of  the  duties and responsibilities of the Board of Directors.

The KPIs used to assess the performance of the Board of Directors' include the following:

1. Financial Performance;
2. Customer Satisfaction;
3. Governance and Compliance; and
4. Human Resources Management.

The  results  of  assesment  will  be  summarized  in  a  report and submitted to the Board of Commissioners, to be further submitted to shareholders through the GMS.

## Assessment on the Committees under the Board of Directors

Currently,  the  Company  has  not  established  committees under the Board of Directors. Therefore, this report is unable to present any information regarding the committees under the Board of Directors.

## Nominasi dan Remunerasi Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi

Nomination and Remuneration of the Board of Commissioners and the Board of Directors

## Prosedur Nominasi

Prosedur penetapan nominasi calon anggota Dewan Komisaris  dan  Direksi  diawali  dengan  pengajuan  kandidat, dilanjutkan  dengan  proses  seleksi  kandidat  calon  anggota Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi. Hasil seleksi kandidat tersebut akan disampaikan kepada pemegang saham utama. Kemudian,  pemegang  saham  akan  mengambil  keputusan dan menetapkan komposisi susunan anggota Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi melalui RUPS.

Dalam menominasikan anggota Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi, Perseroan memerhatikan kriteria yang telah ditetapkan  dalam  POJK.  Kandidat  Dewan  Komisaris  dan Direksi harus memenuhi persyaratan minimum sebagaimana yang disyaratkan regulator, antara lain:

1. Mempunyai akhlak, moral, dan integritas yang baik;
2. Cakap melakukan perbuatan hukum;
3. Dalam 5 (lima) tahun sebelum pengangkatan dan selama menjabat:
- a. tidak pernah dinyatakan pailit;
- b. tidak  pernah  menjadi  anggota  Dewan  Komisaris dan/atau anggota Direksi yang dinyatakan bersalah menyebabkan suatu perusahaan dinyatakan pailit;
- c. tidak  pernah  dihukum  karena  melakukan  tindak pidana  yang  merugikan  keuangan  negara  dan/atau yang berkaitan dengan sektor keuangan; dan
- d. tidak pernah menjadi anggota Dewan Komisaris dan/ atau anggota Direksi yang selama menjabat:
- i. pernah tidak menyelenggarakan RUPST;
- ii. pertanggungjawabannya sebagai anggota Dewan Komisaris dan/atau anggota Direksi pernah  tidak  diterima  oleh  RUPS  atau  pernah tidak memberikan pertanggungjawaban sebagai anggota Dewan  Komisaris dan/atau anggota Direksi kepada RUPS; dan
- iii.  pernah menyebabkan perusahaan yang memperoleh izin, persetujuan, atau pendaftaran dari OJK tidak memenuhi kewajiban menyampaikan laporan tahunan dan/atau laporan keuangan kepada OJK.
4. Memiliki komitmen untuk mematuhi peraturan perundang-undangan; dan
5. Memiliki pengetahuan dan/atau keahlian di bidang yang dibutuhkan oleh Perseroan.

<!-- image -->

## Nomination Procedure

The procedure for determining the nomination of candidates of the Board of Commissioners and Board of Directors begins with the submission of candidates, followed by the selection process for candidates of the Board of Commissioners and Board of Directors. The results of the selection of candidates will be submitted to the majority shareholder. Furthermore, the  shareholders  will  make  decisions  and  determine  the composition of the members of the Board of Commissioners and the Board of Directors through the GMS.

In nominating a member of the Board of Commissioners and the Board of Directors, the Company takes into account the criteria set out in the Financial Services Authority regulation. Candidates for the Board of Commissioners and the Board of Directors shall meet the minimum requirements as required by the regulator, as follows:

1. Have good character, morale, and integrity;
2. Capable of conduct legally competent actions;
3. Within 5 (five) years before appointment and during his tenure:
- a. never been declared bankrupt;
- b. never been Board of Commissioners member and/or Board of Directors member who were found guilty of causing a company to go bankrupt;
- c. never  been  convicted  of  a  criminal  offense  that  is detrimental to the country's finances and/or related to the financial sector; and
- d. never been Board of Commissioners member and/or Board of Directors member who during his/ her term of services:
- i. ever not holding an AGM;
- ii. responsibilities as Board of Commissioners member and/or Board of Directors member were not  accepted  by  the  GMS  or  did  not  provide responsibility as Board of Commissioners member and/or Board of Directors member to the GMS; and
- iii.  has caused a company that obtained permission, approval,  or  registration  from  OJK  to  not  fulfill its obligation to submit an annual report and/or financial report to OJK.
4. Has a commitment to comply with laws and regulations; and
5. Possess knowledge and/or expertise in the field required by the Company.

<!-- image -->

## Prosedur Remunerasi

Mengacu pada Undang-Undang No. 40 Tahun 2007 tentang Perseroan Terbatas, remunerasi Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi ditentukan  oleh  RUPS.  Sesuai  dengan  kewenangan  yang dimilikinya, RUPS  dapat  mendelegasikan  wewenangnya kepada Dewan Komisaris untuk menentukan jumlah remunerasi Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi setiap tahunnya.

Penentuan  gaji,  honorarium,  dan/atau  tunjangan  anggota Direksi telah dijalankan sesuai dengan  POJK  No.  34/ POJK.04/2014  tentang  Komite  Nominasi  dan  Remunerasi Emiten atau Perusahaan Publik, sedangkan gaji dan tunjangan kepada Dewan Komisaris ditentukan oleh RUPS. Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi tidak memperoleh komisi atas kehadiran mereka dalam RUPS.

Pemberian remunerasi harus memperhatikan kinerja masing-masing anggota Dewan Komisaris dan kemampuan Perseroan, serta disesuaikan dengan peraturan perundangundangan yang berlaku.

## Besaran Remunerasi

Total gaji, tunjangan dan tantiem seluruh Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi untuk tahun 2022 adalah sebesar Rp9,7 miliar.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Remuneration Procedure

In  accordance  with  the  Law  No.  40  of  2007  concerning Limited Liability Companies, the remuneration of the Board of Commissioners and the Board of Directors is determined by the GMS. Based on its authority, the GMS may delegate its authority to the Board of Commissioners to determine the amount of remuneration for the Board of Commissioners and the Board of Directors each year.

Determination of salaries, fees, and/or allowances for members of the Board of Directors are in accordance with Financial Services Authority regulation No. 34/POJK.04/2014 concerning  the  Nomination  and  Remuneration  Committee for Issuers or Public Companies. Meanwhile, the salary and allowances for the Board of Commissioners are determined by GMS. The Board of Commissioners and Board of Directors do not receive commissions for their attendance at the GMS.

The allocation  of  remuneration  shall  take  into  account  the performance of each member of the Board of Commissioners and the Company's ability, in compliance with the applicable laws and regulations.

## Remuneration Amount

Total  salaries,  allowances  and  bonuses  for  all  the  Board of  Commissioners  and  the  Board  of  Directors  in  2022  are amounted to Rp9.7billion.

## Organ Pendukung Dewan Komisaris

Supporting Organs of the Board of Commissioners

## KOMITE AUDIT

Perseroan  telah  membentuk  Komite  Audit  sesuai  dengan ketentuan peraturan perundangan yang berlaku dan pembentukannya  dilakukan  berdasarkan  Surat  Keputusan Dewan  Komisaris  PT  Champ  Resto  Indonesia  Tbk  No. 016/CRI-COMM/X/2021  tanggal  12  Oktober  2021  tentang Pembentukan  Komite  Audit  PT  Champ  Resto  Indonesia Tbk.  Surat  Keputusan  tersebut  merujuk  kepada  POJK  No. 55/POJK.04/2015 tentang Pembentukan dan Pedoman Pelaksanaan Kerja Komite Audit.

## AUDIT COMMITTEE

The Company has established Audit Committee in accordance with the prevailing laws  and the establishment is  based  on  the  Decree  of  the  Board  of  Commissioners  of PT Champ Resto Indonesia Tbk No. 016/CRI-COMM/X/2021 dated 12 th  October 2021 regarding the Establishment of the Audit  Committee  of  PT  Champ  Resto  Indonesia  Tbk.  The Decree  refers  to  the  FSA  regulation  No.  55/POJK.04/2015 concerning the Establishment and Guidelines for the Work Implementation of the Audit Committee.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Tugas dan Tanggung Jawab

Tugas dan tanggung jawab Komite Audit adalah:

1. Melakukan  penelaahan  atas  informasi  keuangan  yang akan  dikeluarkan  Perseroan  kepada  publik  dan/atau pihak  otoritas,  antara  lain  laporan  keuangan,  proyeksi dan  laporan  lain  terkait  dengan  informasi  keuangan Perseroan.
2. Melakukan penelaahan atas ketaatan terhadap peraturan perundang-undangan yang berhubungan dengan kegiatan Perseroan.
3. Memberi pendapat independen ketika terjadi perbedaan pendapat antara manajemen dan akuntan atas jasa yang diberikannya.
4. Memberi rekomendasi kepada Dewan Komisaris mengenai  penunjukan  akuntan  yang  didasarkan  pada independensi,  ruang  lingkup  penugasan  dan  imbalan jasa.
5. Melakukan  penelaahan  atas  pelaksanaan  pemeriksaan oleh auditor internal dan mengawasi pelaksanaan tidak lanjut oleh Direksi atas temuan auditor internal.
6. Melakukan  penelaahan  terhadap  aktivitas  pelaksanaan manajemen risiko yang dilakukan oleh Direksi.
7. Menelaah  pengaduan  yang  berkaitan  dengan  proses akuntansi dan pelaporan keuangan Perseroan.
8. Menelaah independensi dan objektivitas akuntan publik.
9. Melakukan  penelaahan  atas  kecukupan  pemeriksaan yang dilakukan oleh akuntan publik.
10.  Melakukan pemeriksaan terhadap dugaan adanya kesalahan dalam keputusan rapat Direksi atau penyimpangan dalam pelaksanaan hasil keputusan rapat Direksi.
11.  Menyampaikan laporan hasil penelaahan kepada seluruh anggota Dewan Komisaris Perseroan setelah selesainya laporan  hasil  penelaahan  yang  dilakukan  oleh  Komite Audit.
12.  Menelaah dan memberikan saran kepada Dewan Komisaris terkait dengan adanya potensi benturan kepentingan Perseroan.
13.  Menjaga kerahasiaan dokumen,  data, dan  informasi Perseroan.

## Komposisi Komite Audit

Masa  jabatan  anggota  Komite  Audit  adalah  paling  lama sampai  penutupan  RUPST  ketiga  berikutnya  dan  dapat dipilih kembali hanya untuk 1 (satu) periode berikutnya. Per 31  Desember  2022,  susunan  Komite  Audit  adalah  sebagai berikut:

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Duties and Responsibilities

The  duties  and  responsibilities  of  Audit  Committee  are  as follows:

1. To review the financial information which will be issued by the Company to the public and/or authorities, such as  financial  statements,  projections,  and  other  reports related to financial information of the Company.
2. To  review  the  Company's  compliance  to  the  laws  and regulations related to the Company's activities.
3. To provide independent opinions in the case of opinion inconsistency  between  management  and  accountant concerning on the service provided.
4. To provide recommendation to the Board of Commissioners on the accountant appointment with the independence, scope of work, and compensation for the services.
5. To review audit implementation by internal auditor and to supervise the implementation of the follow-up by the Board of Directors on the findings of the internal auditor.
6. To review the activities of the risk management implementation carried out by the Board of Directors.
7. To  review  any  complaints  regarding  the  process  of accounting and the Company's financial reporting.
8. To  review  the  independence  and  objectivity  of  public accountants.
9. To review the adequacy of the audit conducted by public accountants.
10.  To  conduct  audit  on  suspicious  of  errors  in  Board of Directors meeting's resolution or deviation of implementation of Board of Directors meeting's resolution.
11. To provide a review report to all members of the Board of  Commissioners  after  the  completion  of  reviewing conduct of audit by the Audit Committee.
12.  To review and provide advise to the Board of Commissioners regarding potential  conflicts  of  interest in the Company.
13.  To maintain the confidentiality of the Company's documents, data, and information.

The  term  of  service  of  members  of  the  Audit  Committee is no later than the closing of the following third AGM and can be re-elected only for 1 (one) subsequent period. As of 31 st   December  2022,  the  composition  of  Audit  Committee was as follows:

## Pernyataan Independensi Komite Audit

Dalam menjalankan tugas dan tanggung jawabnya, seluruh anggota  Komite  Audit  berkomitmen  untuk  menjunjung prinsip  GCG  dengan  bersikap  objektif,  professional,  dan indenpenden. Komite Audit tidak akan mengambil keputusan di  bawah  tekanan  maupun  intervensi  dari  pihak  manapun serta senantiasa menghindari adanya benturan kepentingan.

Seluruh anggota Komite Audit Perseroan merupakan pihak independen yang tidak memiliki hubungan afiliasi keuangan, kepemilikan  saham  dan/atau  hubungan  keluarga  dengan pemegang saham utama, Dewan Komisaris maupun Direksi.

<!-- image -->

## Independence Statement of Audit Committee

In performing of their duties and responsibilities, all members of  the  Audit  Committee  are  committed  to  adhere  GCG principles by being objective, professional, and independent. The Audit Committee will not make decisions under pressure or intervention from any party and always avoid conflicts of interest.

All members  of  the Company's  Audit Committee  are independent parties who have no financial affiliation, share ownership  and/or  family  relationship  with  the  majority shareholders, the Board of Commissioners or the Board of Directors.

Rapat Komite  Audit  hanya  dapat  dilaksanakan  apabila  dihadiri oleh lebih dari ½ (satu per dua) jumlah anggota. ## Piagam Komite Audit

Komite Audit memiliki Piagam Komite Audit sebagai panduan  pelaksanaan  tugas  sesuai  kepatuhan  pada  POJK No. 55/POJK.04/2015 tentang Pembentukan dan Pedoman Pelaksanaan  Kerja  Komite  Audit.  Dewan  Komisaris  telah menyetujui dan mengesahkan Piagam Komite Audit pada 12 Oktober 2021 berdasarkan Surat Keputusan Dewan Komisaris PT Champ Resto Indonesia Tbk No. 017/CRI-COMM/X/2021 tentang  Pembentukan  Piagam  Komite  Audit  PT  Champ Resto Indonesia Tbk.

## Pelaksanaan Kegiatan Komite Audit

Sepanjang  tahun  2022,  Komite  Audit  telah  melaksanakan hal-hal berikut ini:

1. Mengkaji  dan  memberikan  pendapat  atas  hasil  audit laporan keuangan untuk tahun buku 2021 sesuai dengan standar audit yang berlaku;
2. Memberikan rekomendasi mengenai penunjukan Akuntan  Publik  dan  Kantor  Akuntan  Publik  kepada Dewan Komisaris sebagai pelimpahan wewenang RUPS;
3. Menelaah hasil laporan keuangan setiap triwulan sebelum dipublikasikan;
4. Melakukan penelaahan atas kepatuhan Perseroan terhadap peraturan perundang-undangan yang berhubungan dengan perusahaan terbuka;
5. Membahas isu-isu terhadap laporan keuangan (jika ada) dan  menelaah  efektif  kinerja  auditor  eksternal  (KAP) sesuai dengan kemandirian dan objektivitasnya.

## Charter of Audit Committee

The  Audit  Committee  has  an  Audit  Committee  Charter as  a  guide  to  execute  their  duties  in  accordance  with the Financial Services Authority regulation No. 55/ POJK.04/2015 concerning the Establishment and Guidelines for the Work Implementation of the Audit Committee. The Board  of  Commissioners  has  approved  and  ratified  the Audit Committee Charter on 12 th  October 2021 based on the Decree of the Board of Commissioners of PT Champ Resto Indonesia  Tbk  No.  017/CRI-COMM/X/2021  concerning  the Establishment of the  Audit Committee Charter of PT Champ Resto Indonesia Tbk.

## Implementation of Audit Committee

Throughout 2022, the Audit Committee managed to carry out its duties as follows:

1. Reviewed and provided an opinion on the results of the audit of the financial statements for the Fiscal Year 2021 in accordance with the applicable auditing standards;
2. Provided recommendations regarding the appointment of a Public Accountant and Public Accounting Firm to the Board of Commissioners as a delegation of the authority of the GMS;
3. Reviewed financial statement reports quarterly prior to publication;
4. Reviewed the Company's compliance with the applicable laws and regulations;
5. Discussed  the  issues  of  financial  statement    (if  any) and  reviewed  the  effectiveness  of  the  performance of  the  external  auditor,  the  Public  Accounting  Firm,  in accordance with their independence and objectivity.

Supporting Organs of the Board of Directors

## SEKRETARIS PERUSAHAAN

Berdasarkan POJK No. 35/POJK.04/2014 tentang Sekretaris Perusahaan Emiten atau Perusahaan Publik, Sekretaris Perusahaan merupakan organ pendukung Perseroan yang  berperan  penting  dalam  memfasilitasi  komunikasi antar  organ  Perseroan,  hubungan  antara  Perseroan  dan pemegang saham, regulator seperti OJK, BEI, dan pemangku kepentingan  lainnya.  Sekretaris  Perusahaan  bertanggung jawab  dalam  memastikan  kepatuhan  Perseroan  terhadap peraturan perundang-undangan di bidang pasar modal.

Sekretaris Perusahaan diangkat oleh Direktur Utama dengan persetujuan Dewan Komisaris. Berdasarkan Surat Keputusan Direksi PT Champ  Resto  Indonesia Tbk No.  020/CRIDIR/X/2021 tanggal 12 Oktober 2021 tentang Pengangkatan Sekretaris Perusahaan PT Champ Resto Indonesia Tbk, Bapak Christopher  Supit  diangkat  sebagai  Sekretaris  Perusahaan. Beliau juga menjabat sebagai Direktur Perseroan.

<!-- image -->

## CORPORATE SECRETARY

As stipulated in the Financial Services Authority regulation No.  35/POJK.04/2014  concerning  Corporate  Secretary  of Issuers  or  Public  Companies,  the  Corporate  Secretary  is  a supporting organ of the Company that liases an important role  in  facilitating  communication  between  the  Company's organs, the Relationship between the Company and shareholders,  regulators  such  as  the  FSA,  IDX,  and  other stakeholders.  The  Corporate  Secretary  is  responsible  for ensuring  the  Company's  compliance  with  the  laws  and Regulations in the capital market sector.

The  Corporate  Secretary  is  appointed  by  the  President Director  upon  approval  of  the  Board  of  Commissioners. Based on the Decree of the Board of Directors of PT  Champ  Resto  Indonesia  Tbk  No.  020/CRI-DIR/X/2021  dated 12 th   October 2021 regarding the Appointment of the Corporate Secretary of PT Champ Resto Indonesia Tbk, Mr. Christopher Supit was appointed as the Company's Corporate Secretary. He also serves as the Company's Director.

## Tugas dan Tanggung Jawab

Sekretaris  Perusahaan  mempunyai  tugas  dan  tanggung jawab sebagai berikut:

1. Mengikuti perkembangan Pasar Modal, khususnya peraturan perundang-undangan yang berlaku di bidang pasar modal.
2. Memberi masukan kepada Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi untuk mematuhi peraturan perundang-undangan di bidang pasar modal.
3. Membantu Direksi dan Dewan Komisaris dalam pelaksanaan tata kelola perusahaan yang meliputi:
- a. keterbukaan informasi kepada masyarakat, termasuk ketersediaan informasi pada situs web Perseroan;
- b. penyampaian  laporan kepada OJK  secara tepat waktu;
- c. penyelenggaraan dan dokumentasi RUPS;
- d. penyelenggaraan dan dokumentasi rapat Direksi dan/atau Dewan Komisaris; dan
- e. pelaksanaan program orientasi terhadap perusahaan bagi Direksi dan/atau Dewan Komisaris.
4. Sebagai penghubung antara Perseroan dengan pemegang saham Perseroan, regulator,  dan  pemangku kepentingan lainnya.
5. Menjaga kerahasiaan dokumen, data dan informasi yang  bersifat  rahasia  kecuali  dalam  rangka  memenuhi kewajiban sesuai dengan peraturan perundangundangan atau ditentukan lain dalam peraturan perundang-undangan.
6. Mengikuti pendidikan dan/atau pelatihan dalam rangka meningkatkan pengetahuan dan pemahaman  untuk membantu pelaksanaan tugasnya.
7. Membuat laporan secara berkala sekurang-kurangnya 1 (satu) kali dalam setahun mengenai pelaksanaan fungsi Sekretaris Perusahaan kepada Direksi dan ditembuskan kepada Dewan Komisaris.

His  profile  has  been  disclosed  in  the  Company  Profile chapter,  the  Board  of  Directors  Profile  sub  section  in  this Annual Report.

## Duties and Responsibilities

The duties and responsibilities of Corporate Secretary are as follows:

1. To  closely  monitor  the  development  of  capital  market, particularly with regards to applicable laws and regulations of the capital market sector.
2. To provide inputs to the Board of Commissioners and the Board of Directors concerning compliance with laws and regulations in the capital market.
3. To assist the Board of Directors and Board of Commissioners in corporate governance which includes:
- a. public  information  disclosure,  including  information availability on the Company's website;
- b. timely report submission to the FSA;
- c. organization and documentation of the GMS;
- d. organization and documentation of Board of Directors  and/or  Board  of  Commissioners  meeting; and
- e. organization of orientation program of the Company for  the  Board  of  Directors  and/or  the  Board  of Commissioners.
4. To  act  as  a  liaison  officer  or  contact  person  between the  Company  and  shareholders,  regulators,  and  other stakeholders.
5. To maintain  the confidentiality  of  documents,  data and confidential information except  the  Company's obligations in  accordance with laws and regulations or otherwise stipulated in laws and regulations;
6. To  participate  in  education  and/or  training  in  order to  enhance  knowledge  and  understanding  to  assist  in carrying out the duties.
7. To submit periodic reports at least once a year regarding the implementation of the Corporate Secretary function to the Board of Directors and the Board of Commissioners as well.

## Pelaksanaan Kegiatan Sekretaris Perusahaan

Pada 2022, Sekretaris Perusahaan telah melakukan hal-hal berikut ini:

1. Mengelola administrasi pemegang saham Perseroan.
2. Melakukan aktivitas komunikasi yang bersifat korporasi kepada  pemangku  kepentingan  baik  internal  maupun eksternal Perseroan.
3. Melakukan keterbukaan informasi atau pelaporan sesuai dengan ketentuan perundang-undangan kepada regulator yang menjadi kewenangan Sekretaris Perseroan sehubungan dengan status Perseroan sebagai perusahaan terbuka.
4. Menyelenggarakan dan mengadministrasikan RUPS.
5. Menyelenggarakan dan mengadministrasikan rapatrapat Direksi dan Dewan Komisaris Perseroan.
6. Melakukan penyesuaian kebijakan Perseroan terkait tata kelola perusahaan.
7. Melakukan analyst meeting dengan market analyst serta calon investor.
8. Melaksanakan  fungsi kepatuhan Perseroan terhadap peraturan di bidang pasar modal.

## Hubungan Investor

Hubungan investor merupakan bagian dari Sekretaris Perusahaan yang berperan aktif dalam menjalin komunikasi antara Perseroan dan para pemegang saham, broker, institusi, manajer  investasi,  dan  analis  dalam  rangka  membahas pencapaian dan rencana Perseroan. Komunikasi dilaksanakan secara  rutin  melalui analyst  meeting ,  pertemuan  dengan investor potensial atau calon investor, konferensi pers, serta paparan publik.

Komunikasi dua arah ini memberikan akses informasi yang luas terkait kinerja Perseroan bagi komunitas investasi dan para  pemangku  kepentingan  lainnya.    Selain  itu,  proses komunikasi  ini  dapat  menggambarkan  dan  menyediakan pandangan yang akurat terkait informasi terkini Perseroan, sehingga  dapat  memberikan  citra  positif  bagi  Perseroan. Seluruh  kebijakan  mengenai  hubungan  investor  tertuang dalam Kebijakan Komunikasi dengan Pemegang Saham atau Investor.

## UNIT AUDIT INTERNAL

Unit Audit Internal adalah unit kerja dan organ pendukung Perseroan  yang  bekerja  secara  independen  dan  objektif, dengan tujuan untuk meningkatkan nilai dan memperbaiki operasional Perseroan melalui pendekatan yang sistematis, dengan  cara  mengevaluasi  dan  meningkatkan  efektivitas manajemen risiko, pengendalian, serta memperkuat penerapan praktik GCG.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Corporate Secretary Activities

In 2022, the Corporate Secretary has carried out its duties as follows:

1. Managed the Company's shareholder administration.
2. Carried out corporate communications with internal and external stakeholders.
3. Ensured  the  implementation  of  information  disclosure and reporting in accordance with regulatory provisions under  the authority of the Corporate  Secretary in connection  with  the  Company's  status  as  a  public company.
4. Organized the GMS.
5. Organized the meetings of the Board of Directors and the Board of Commissioners.
6. Amended the Company's policies concerning corporate governance.
7. Conducted  analyst  meetings  with  market  analysts  and potential investors.
8. Ensured the Company's compliance with the applicable laws and regulations in the capital market sector.

## Investor Relations

Investor relations is part of duties of the Corporate Secretary who plays a key role in establishing communication between the Company and shareholders, brokers, institutions, investment managers, and analysts to discuss the Company's achievements and plans. Communication shall be carried out on a regular basis through analyst meetings, meetings with potential  or  prospective  investors,  press  conferences,  and public exposure.

This  particular  two-way  communication  provides  a  wide access to information related to the Company's performance for  the  investment  community  and  stakeholders.  Besides, this  communication  process  is  capable  of  explaining  and providing accurate opinions regarding the Company's latest information with the aim of maintaining the positive image. All policies related to investor relations can be found in the Shareholder and Investor Communication Policy.

## INTERNAL AUDIT UNIT

The  Internal  Audit  Unit  is  an  independent  work  unit  and supporting organ who works independently and objectively, to  enhance  values  and  improve  the  Company's  operations in a systematic approach, by evaluating and improving the effectiveness of risk management, control, and strengthening the implementation of GCG practice.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Pembentukan  dan  pelaksanaan  tugas  Unit  Audit  Internal Perseroan mengacu pada:

<!-- image -->

The establishment and implementation of the duties of the Company's Internal Audit Unit refers to:

- POJK No. 56/POJK.04/2015 tentang Pembentukan dan Pedoman Penyusunan Piagam Unit Audit Internal.

Perseroan  senantiasa  mendorong  aktivitas  pengembangan unit audit internal melalui berbagai program pengembangan kompetensi terkait perkembangan bisnis terkini. Pada tahun 2022, pengembangan kompetensi audit Internal Perseroan dilakukan secara mandiri melalui media buku serta informasi digital.

## Training of Internal Audit Unit

On an ongoing basis, the Company improves the competency development for the Internal Audit Unit through a number of competency development programs related to the latest  business  developments.  In  2022,  the  competency development  programs  for  the  Internal  Audit  Unit  were carried out independently through books and digital books.

## Struktur dan Kedudukan

Unit  Audit  Internal  Perseroan  diangkat  dan  diberhentikan oleh  Direktur  Utama,  serta  bertanggung  jawab  kepada Direktur Utama.

2

0

## Structure and Position

The Company's Internal Audit Unit is appointed and dismissed by the President Director, and also answers to the President Director.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Tugas dan Tanggung Jawab

Unit Audit Internal mempunyai tugas dan tanggung jawab sebagai berikut:

1. Menyusun  dan  melaksanakan  rencana  Audit  Internal tahunan;
2. Menguji  dan  mengevaluasi  pelaksanaan  pengendalian interen dan sistem manajemen sesuai dengan kebijakan Perseroan;
3. Melakukan pemeriksaan dan penilaian atas efisiensi dan efektivitas  di  bidang  keuangan,  akuntansi,  operasional, sumber  daya  manusia,  pemasaran,  teknologi  informasi dan kegiatan lainnya;
4. Memberikan saran perbaikan dan informasi yang objektif tentang  kegiatan  yang  diperiksa  pada  semua  tingkat manajemen;
5. Membuat laporan hasil audit dan menyampaikan laporan tersebut kepada Direktur Utama dan Dewan Komisaris;
6. Memantau,  menganalisa  dan  melaporkan  pelaksanaan tindak lanjut yang telah disarankan;
7. Bekerja sama dengan Komite Audit;
8. Menyusun program untuk mengevaluasi mutu kegiatan Unit Audit Internal yang dilakukannya; dan
9. Melakukan pemeriksaan khusus apabila diperlukan.

## Piagam Unit Audit Internal

Perseroan  telah  menyusun  Piagam  Audit  Internal (Internal Audit  Charter) yang  tercantum  dalam  Surat  Keputusan Direksi No. 022/CRI-DIR/X/2021 tertanggal 21 Oktober 2021 tentang Piagam Audit Internal dengan merujuk pada POJK No. 56/POJK.04/2015 tentang Pembentukan dan Pedoman Penyusunan Piagam Unit Audit Internal.

## Rapat Unit Audit Internal

Unit Audit Internal wajib mengadakan rapat secara berkala dengan  Direksi,  Dewan  Komisaris,  dan/atau  Komite  Audit. Pelaksanaan  rapat  juga  dapat  diadakan  sewaktu-waktu (insidental)  apabila  diminta  oleh  Dewan  Komisaris  atau terdapat  kejadian  penting  dan  mendesak  yang  terjadi  di bidang area pengawasannya.

Pada 2022, Unit Audit Internal telah mengadakan 4 (empat) kali  rapat  dengan  Komite  Audit  dan  1  (satu)  kali  rapat bersama Komite Audit dan Direksi.

## Pelaksanaan Tugas Unit Audit Internal

Sepanjang 2022, Unit Audit Internal telah menjalankan tugas dan  tanggung  jawabnya  secara  objektif,  independen,  dan profesional. Saat ini, Audit Internal melakukan pemeriksaan terhadap  hal-hal  yang  berkaitan  dengan  risiko  finansial sebagai bentuk upaya pengendalian bahwa seluruh prosedur, proses, dan informasi yang ada telah sesuai dengan ketentuan yang berlaku.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Duties and Responsibilities

The Internal Audit Unit has the following duties  and responsibilities:

1. To prepare and implement the annual Internal Audit plan;
2. To verify and evaluate the implementation of the internal control and management system in compliance with the Company's policy;
3. To examine and assess efficiency and effectiveness in the field of finance, accounting, operational, human capital, marketing, information technology and other activities;
4. To provide advice for improvement and objective information on activities examined  at all levels of management;
5. To prepare audit report and submit such report to the President Director and the Board of Commissioners;
6. To monitor, analyze and report on the implementation of recommended follow-up action;
7. To cooperate with the Audit Committee;
8. To develop program for the evaluation of the quality of Internal Audit Unit activities; and
9. To conduct special examination if required.

## Charter of Internal Audit Unit

The  Company  has  prepared  an  Internal  Audit  Charter  as stipulated  in  the  Board  of  Directors'  Decree  No.  022/CRIDIR/X/2021 dated October 21, 2021, concerning the Internal Audit  Charter  with  reference  to  the  FSA  Number  56/ POJK.04/2015 concerning the Establishment and Guidelines for Drafting the Internal Audit Unit Charter.

## Meeting of Internal Audit Unit

The Internal Audit Unit is required to conduct regular meetings with the Board of Directors, Board of Commissioners, and/ or the Audit Committee. Meetings can be conducted at any time (incidental) if requested by Board of Commissioners or if there are important and urgent in the supervision area.

In 2022, the Internal Audit Unit has held 4 (four) meetings with  Audit  Committee  and  1  (one)  time  meeting  with  the Audit Committee and the Board of Directors.

## Implementation of Internal Audit Unit Duties

Throughout  2022,  the  Internal  Audit  Unit  managed  to carry  out  its  duties  and  responsibilities  in  an  objective, independent  and  professional  manner.  Currently,  Internal Audit  is  examining  the  issues  related  to  financial  risks  as an effort  to  ensure  that  all  existing  procedures,  processes, and  information  are  in  compliance  with  the  applicable regulations.

<!-- image -->

## SISTEM PENGENDALIAN INTERNAL

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## INTERNAL CONTROL SYSTEM

## Pengendalian Keuangan dan Operasional

Sistem  pengendalian  internal  yang  komprehensif,  efektif, dan efisien sangat diperlukan untuk mendukung pencapaian tujuan  Perseroan.  Penerapan  sistem  pengendalian  internal dimulai  dari  penetapan  sasaran  dan  strategi  di  seluruh organisasi, identifikasi kemungkinan terjadinya suatu kejadian  yang  dapat  memengaruhi  sasaran  dan  strategi dimaksud, dan memitigasi risiko yang ada dengan tepat.

Tujuan sistem pengendalian internal Perseroan secara komprehensif adalah sebagai berikut:

1. Menjaga dan mengamankan harta kekayaan Perseroan.
2. Menjamin tersedianya laporan yang lebih akurat.
3. Meningkatkan kepatuhan terhadap ketentuan yang berlaku.
4. Mengurangi dampak kerugian keuangan, termasuk kecurangan,  dan  pelanggaran  terhadap  prinsip  kehatihatian.
5. Meningkatkan efektivitas organisasi dan efisiensi biaya.

Sistem pengendalian internal Perseroan juga mengharuskan adanya  upaya  untuk  memelihara  catatan  dan  menyajikan laporan  keuangan  sesuai  prinsip-prinsip  akuntansi  yang berlaku, serta mewajibkan pengungkapan seluruh transaksi material yang memengaruhi perubahan nilai aset, kewajiban dan  modal.  Hal  tersebut  perlu  dilakukan  sebagai  upaya mencegah aksi kecurangan dan pencegahan penyelewengan keuangan.

## Kepatuhan Terhadap Peraturan Perundang-Undangan

Perseroan berkomitmen kuat untuk senantiasa menjalankan praktik  bisnis  sehat  yang  mematuhi  peraturan  perundangundangan yang berlaku di Indonesia. Untuk itu,  Perseroan secara  berkala  melakukan  evaluasi  dan  memperbaharui proses bisnis dan aturan internalnya sesuai dengan perubahan peraturan perundang-undangan yang berlaku.

Pengendalian internal diterapkan melalui kebijakan, regulasi dan prosedur yang jelas sehingga dapat meminimalisir risiko usaha dan mengoptimalkan kinerja Perseroan, yang antara lain terdiri dari:

- a. Penerapan praktik GCG;
- b. Penerapan fungsi pengawasan di masing-masing divisi;
- c. Pelaksanaan tugas pendampingan terhadap KAP; dan
- d. Pemantauan  dan  pelaksanaan  tindak  lanjut  terhadap temuan-temuan audit.

2

0

## Financial and Operational Control

A  comprehensive,  effective,  and  efficient  internal  control system  is  necessary  to  support  the  Company's  objectives. The  implementation  of  internal  control  system  starts  with determining  the  goals  and  strategies  in  all  organizational levels, identifying possibilities that may affect the goals and strategies, and mitigating the existing risks correctly.

The objectives of comprehensive internal control system are as follows:

1. To maintain and secure the Company's assets.
2. To provide the accurate reports.
3. To improve compliance with applicable regulations.
4. To  minimize  the  impact  of  financial  losses,  including fraud, and violations of the prudential principle.
5. To improve organizational effectiveness and cost efficiency.

The  Company's  internal  control  system  requires  an  action to  maintain  records  and  financial  statement  based  on applicable accounting principles, including disclosure of all material transactions that affect changes in assets, liabilities and  capital.  These  rules  and  policies  are  needed  to  avoid frauds and prevent financial misappropriation.

## Compliance with Rules and Regulations

The Company is committed to carrying out sound business in  compliance  with  the  applicable  laws  and  regulations  in Indonesia.  Therefore,  the  Company  keeps  evaluating  and updating its business operations and internal regulations in accordance with the prevailing laws and regulations.

Internal control shall be implemented through clear policies, regulations,  and  procedures  in  order  to  minimize  business risks and optimize the Company's performance, as follows:

- a. Implementation of GCG principles;
- b. Implementation of supervisory function in each division;
- c. Assisting the Public Accounting Firm; and
- d. Monitoring and following up on audit findings.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Tinjauan atas Efektivitas Sistem Pengendalian Internal

Sistem pengendalian internal harus terus dijalankan dengan disiplin dan ketat untuk menjaga stabilitas kinerja Perseroan, baik  secara  operasional  dan  keuangan.  Untuk  itu,  setiap divisi dan organ tata kelola wajib menjalankan peran, tugas, dan tanggung jawabnya secara optimal dan konsisten agar terlaksana sebuah sistem yang efektif dan efisien.

## Pernyataan Direksi atas Kecukupan Sistem Pengendalian Internal

Berdasarkan  evaluasi  dan  tinjauan  Direksi  atas  penerapan sistem  pengendalian  internal  Perseroan  sepanjang  2022, Direksi  menyatakan  bahwa  Perseroan  telah  melaksanakan sistem  pengendalian  internal  secara  memadai,  sehingga kegiatan usaha Perseroan dapat berjalan dengan baik dan terkendali.  Penilaian  ini  didukung  dengan  tidak  ditemukan pelanggaran signifikan yang dapat membawa kerugian bagi Perseroan.

Direksi  senantiasa  mengembangkan  sistem  pengendalian internal sesuai dengan perkembangan Perseroan agar sistem pengendalian internal mampu terlaksana dengan optimal.

## SISTEM MANAJEMEN RISIKO

Dalam menjalankan usahanya, Perseroan tentunya menghadapi  berbagai  tantangan  dan  risiko  baik  internal dan  eksternal.  Seiring  dengan  perkembangan  dunia  usaha dan  kinerja  industri,  Perseroan  akan  menemui  risiko-risiko baru baik secara langsung dan tidak langsung. Oleh sebab itu, Perseroan menilai penting penerapan sistem manajemen risiko yang bersifat preventif dan antisipatif, yang merupakan salah satu aspek penting dalam praktik GCG. Sistem manajemen risiko yang terstruktur dan andal memampukan Perseroan untuk mendeteksi, menganalisis, mencegah, dan memitigasi segala bentuk risiko yang dapat terjadi.

## PROFIL RISIKO

<!-- image -->

## Overview  of  the  Effectiveness  of  Internal  Control System

The  internal  control  system  shall  be  implemented  in  a disciplined and strict manner in order to maintain the stability of  the  Company's  operational  and  financial  performance. Hence,  each  division  and  organ  of  governance  shall  carry out  its  roles,  duties,  and  responsibilities  optimally  and consistently, with the aim of implementing an effective and efficient system.

## Statement of the Board of Directors on the Adequacy of Internal Control System

According to the Board of Directors' evaluation and review on  the  implementation  of  the  Company's  internal  control system  in  2022,  the  Company  managed  to  implement  an adequate  internal  control  system,  so  that  the  Company's operations were under control. Furthermore, no significant violations  that  could  bring  losses  to  the  Company  were reported.

The  Board  of  Directors  consistently  improves  the  internal control  system  in  line  with  the  Company's  development so  that  the  internal  control  system  can  be  implemented optimally.

## RISK MANAGEMENT SYSTEM

In carrying out its operations, the Company constantly deals with  numerous  internal  and  external  challenges  and  risks. In  the  course  of  business  and  industry  development,  the Company will encounter new risks both direct and indirectly. According to the Company's assessment, the implementation of  preventive  and  anticipatory  risk  management  system is  essential,  which  is  also  an  important  aspect  in  the  GCG implementation. A structured and reliable risk management system allows the Company to detect, analyze, prevent, and mitigate all forms of potential risks.

Risiko likuiditas  Liquidity risk  •  Menjaga arus kas masuk dan keluar agar level likuiditas Perseroan tetap terjaga.  •  Terus berupaya dalam meningkatkan kinerja keuangan, agar level likuiditas Perseroan  dapat terus membaik setelah pandemi COVID-19.  •  Maintaining liquidity level by keeping properly cash inflows and outflows.  •  Improving the financial performance continuously, to create the liquidity level to get  better after the COVID-19 pandemic.  22.  Risiko  ketidakcukupan  asuransi  yang  dimiliki Perseroan  Risk of insufficient insurance  Memastikan bahwa perusahaan asuransi yang dikontrak oleh Perseroan merupakan pihak  yang kredibel dalam membayar klaim.  Ensuring the Company has contract insurance with a credible insurance company in terms  of claim payout.  23.  Risiko atas akuisisi yang mungkin akan  dilakukan di masa mendatang  Risk of possible future acquisitions  Melakukan  due diligence  secara lengkap dan terukur sebelum melakukan akuisisi di masa  mendatang.  Conducting due diligence complately and measurable prior to future acquisitions.   |

## Tinjauan atas Efektivitas Sistem Manajemen Risiko

Perseroan secara berkala meninjau risiko usaha yang relevan dengan mengembangkan dan meningkatkan kerangka manajemen  risiko dan struktur pengendalian internal yang terpadu dan komprehensif. Pengelolaan risiko selalu ditingkatkan dengan mengadaptasi perubahan bisnis  baik  dari  internal  maupun  secara  industri.  Kegiatan ini  harus  dilakukan  secara  berkelanjutan  sehingga  dapat meminimalkan dampak risiko sejak awal.

<!-- image -->

## Review  on  the  Effectiveness  of  Risk  Management System

On a regular basis, the Company reviews relevant business risks by developing and  improving  an integrated  and comprehensive  risk  management  framework  and  internal control structure. Risk management is continuously improved by  adapting  to  changes  in  businesses  both  internally  and industrially, with the aim of minimizing the impact of risk as early as possible.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Kerangka  manajemen  risiko  Perseroan  dituangkan  dalam kebijakan,  prosedur,  deskripsi  pekerjaan,  serta  berbagai perangkat manajemen risiko yang berlaku di seluruh lingkup aktivitas usaha Perseroan.

<!-- image -->

The Company's risk management framework is stipulated in policies, procedures, job descriptions, and risk management tools that are implemented in the Company's operations.

## Pernyataan  Direksi  dan/atau  Dewan  Komisaris  atas Kecukupan Sistem Manajemen Risiko

Direksi  dan  Dewan  Komisaris  secara  berkala  mengevaluasi penerapan sistem manajemen risiko secara rutin. Berbagai upaya mitigasi perlu dirumuskan dengan hati-hati dikarenakan risiko yang ada bergantung juga kepada perkembangan usaha. Berdasarkan hasil evaluasi, sepanjang tahun  2022,  Direksi  dan  Dewan  Komisaris  menilai  bahwa sistem manajemen risiko saat ini sudah berjalan dengan baik dan sistematis. Selain itu, Dewan Komisaris juga menghimbau agar  sistem  manajemen  risiko  senantiasa  diperkuat  dan dioptimalkan agar dapat mengidentifikasi risiko-risiko usaha baru serta memitigasi risiko yang sudah ada dengan lebih tepat.

## PERKARA HUKUM DAN SANKSI ADMINISTRATIF

Sepanjang tahun 2022 dan sampai Laporan Tahunan Terintegrasi  ini  diterbitkan,  Perseroan,  dan  masing-masing anggota Dewan Komisaris maupun Direksi Perseroan tidak  menghadapi  perkara  hukum  yang  mungkin  dapat berpengaruh secara material dan dapat berdampak negatif terhadap kelangsungan usaha.

Sepanjang tahun 2022 dan sampai laporan ini diterbitkan, Perseroan juga tidak mendapatkan sanksi administratif yang diberikan oleh pihak regulator.

## KODE ETIK PERUSAHAAN

Kode  Etik  merupakan  seperangkat  nilai  dan  aturan  bisnis standar  yang  membentuk  pedoman  tata  cara  bekerja, bersikap, dan berperilaku seseorang. Perseroan menetapkan dan  memberlakukan  Kode  Etik  Perusahaan  agar  setiap karyawan  senantiasa  merepresentasikan  reputasi  dan  nilai Perseroan  dalam  kinerja  dan  perilakunya,  yaitu  integritas, tertib  dan  disiplin  yang  menjadi  budaya  Perseroan.  Hal  ini menjadi pedoman bagi seluruh karyawan dalam berinteraksi dan menjalin hubungan dengan para pemangku kepentingan, sehingga menghindari terjadinya penyimpangan yang berpotensi merugikan Perseroan.

Pelaksanaan Kode Etik ini merupakan salah satu komitmen dari Perseroan terhadap prinsip-prinsip GCG, untuk mencapai visi dan misi yang telah ditetapkan.

Dengan diterapkannya Kode Etik, Perseroan berfokus untuk membangun kegiatan bisnis yang sehat, transparan, adil, dan profesional.

0

## Statement of the Board of Directors and/or Board of Commissioners on the Adequacy of Internal Control System

The  Board  of  Directors  and  the  Board  of  Commissioners constantly evaluate the implementation of risk management system.  Mitigation  efforts  are  required  to  be  prudently formulated  as  the  changes  in  risks  are  related  to  the business  development.  According  to  the  evaluation  of  the Board  of  Directors  and  the  Board  of  Commissioners,  the risk  management  system  during  the  year  2022  managed to  operate  in  a  proper  and  systematically.  In  addition,  the Board  of  Commissioners  constantly  ensures  to  strengthen and optimize the risk management system with the aim of identifying new business risks and mitigating existing risks more precisely.

## LEGAL CASES AND ADMINISTRATIVE SANCTIONS

Throughout 2022 and until the publication of this Integrated Annual  Report,  no  legal  cases  with  a  material  effect  and negative impact on business continuity have been brought against the Company, the Board of Commissioners, and the Board of Directors.

Throughout 2022 and until the publication of this report, the Company also did not receive any administrative sanctions from the regulators.

## CODE OF CONDUCT

The  Code  of  Conduct  is  a  set  of  values,  standards,  and principles  that  outlines  the  appropriate  work  procedures, behavior,  and  attitude  in  the  workplace.  The  Company stipulates and implements the Code of Conduct so that all employees continue to represent and reflect the Company's reputation and values in the workplace and their behavior, namely integrity, competency, and discipline which are parts of the Company's culture. The Code of Conduct serves as a guideline for employees to interact and establish relationships with stakeholders, thereby avoiding any potential violations that may harm the Company.

The  implementation  of  Code  of  Conduct  is  part  of  the Company's commitments to the GCG implementation, with the aim of achieving the Company's vision and mission.

Through  the  implementation  of  the  Code  of  Conduct,  the Company focuses on building sound, transparent, fair, and professional business activities.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Pokok-Pokok Kode Etik

Pokok-pokok Kode Etik Perseroan adalah sebagai berikut:

1. Berkesadaran Etik: memiliki komitmen untuk berorientasi kepada pelanggan dalam membangun mitra kerja sama yang  sehat  dengan  tetap  memenuhi  peraturan  yang berlaku.
2. Berperilaku Etik: menerapkan disiplin bekerja dan tidak  menerima  gratifikasi  serta  menerapkan  prinsip antikorupsi, kolusi dan nepotisme.

## Sosialisasi dan Penegakan Kode Etik

Kode Etik  Perseroan  merupakan  bagian  yang  tidak  terpisahkan dengan Peraturan Perusahaan. Dalam pelaksanaannya, Kode Etik  Perseroan  senantiasa  disosialisasikan  secara  berkala kepada seluruh karyawan baik dalam bentuk sharing session maupun melalui berbagai media informasi, seperti email dan whatsapp group .  Kode Etik Perseroan berlaku setara untuk Dewan Komisaris, Direksi, dan seluruh karyawan.

Untuk memastikan efektivitas penerapannya, seluruh anggota Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi telah menandatangani Pedoman dan Kode Etik Direksi dan Dewan Komisaris.

## KEBIJAKAN  KEPEMILIKAN  SAHAM  PERUSAHAAN OLEH DEWAN KOMISARIS DAN/ATAU DIREKSI

Perusahaan telah memiliki kebijakan terkait pengungkapan informasi kepemilikan saham Dewan Komisaris dan Direksi  diterapkan  dengan  mengacu  pada  POJK  No.  11/ POJK.04/2017  tentang  Laporan  Kepemilikan  atau  Setiap Perubahan Kepemilikan Saham Perusahaan Terbuka.

Sejalan  dengan  peraturan  tersebut,  Perseroan  mewajibkan anggota Direksi dan anggota Dewan Komisaris menyampaikan informasi kepada Perseroan dalam hal terjadi perubahan  kepemilikan  atas  saham  Perseroan  maksimal  3 (tiga) hari kerja setelah tanggal transaksi.

Seluruh  saham  yang  dimiliki  anggota  Dewan  Komisaris dan/atau  Direksi  telah  dilaporkan  kepada  regulator  sesuai peraturan yang berlaku.

## SISTEM PELAPORAN PELANGGARAN

Guna  memastikan  pelaksanaan  pengendalian  internal  dan kegiatan  usaha  yang  sehat  serta  berkelanjutan,  Perseroan membentuk sistem pelaporan pelanggaran ( whistleblowing system /WBS).  WBS  merupakan  bagian  tidak  terpisahkan dari pelaksanaan Kode Etik Perseroan.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## The Principles of the Code of Conduct

The principles of the Company's Code of Conduct comprise as follows:

1. Ethical  Awareness:  the  commitment  to  developing  a customer-oriented  business  and  successful  partnership in compliance with the applicable regulations.
2. Behaving Ethically: implementing work discipline, refusing  gratuities,  as  well  as  preventing  corruption, collusion, and nepotism in the workplace.

## Dissemination  and  Enforcement  of  the Code  of Conduct

The  Code  of  Conduct  is  an  integral  part  of  the  Company Regulations, and applies equally to the Board of Commissioners, the Board of Directors, and employees. The Code of Conduct is regularly disseminated to all employees, in  the  form  of  sharing  sessions  and  through  media  of communication,  such  as  email  and  WhatsApp  groups. The Code of Conduct is equally applicable to the Board of Commissioners, the Board of Directors, and all employees.

In an effort to ensure the effectiveness of the implementation of Code of Conduct, all members of the Board of Commissioners and Board of Directors signed the Guidelines and Code of Conduct for the Board of Commissioners and the Board of Directors.

## COMPANY  SHARE  OWNERSHIP  POLICY  BY  THE BOARD  OF  COMMISSIONERS  AND/OR  THE  BOARD OF DIRECTORS

The Company has a policy related to the disclosure of share ownership  of  the  Board  of  Commissioners  and  the  Board of Directors in compliance with the FSA Regulation No. 11/ POJK.04/2017  concerning  Report  of  Ownership  or  Every Amendment in Share Ownership of Public Companies.

In  accordance  with  this  regulation,  the  Company  requires the Board of Directors and the Board of Commissioners to submit information to the Company for the changing of the Company's share ownership no later than 3 (three) working days after the date of transaction.

All shares owned by members of the Board of Commissioners and/or Board of Directors have been reported to the regulator in accordance with the applicable regulations.

## WHISTLEBLOWING SYSTEM

In an effort to ensure the implementation of internal control as well as a sound and sustainable business, the Company established a whistleblowing system (WBS), an integral part of the implementation of the Code of Conduct.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

WBS merupakan mekanisme komunikasi secara rahasia yang memampukan  pelapor  untuk  melaporkan  adanya  indikasi kecurangan  atau  pelanggaran  di  dalam  Perseroan.  Untuk itu, dalam rangka penyelenggaraan praktik GCG, Perseroan menyediakan sarana komunikasi dan pelaporan dalam bentuk  email  maupun  surat  bagi  pihak  internal  ataupun eksternal Perseroan.

<!-- image -->

WBS is a confidential communication mechanism that allows the whistleblowers to report indications of fraud or violations within the Company. Accordingly, in the event to implement the GCG principles, the Company provides communication and  reporting  facilities  in  the  form  of  email  and  letter  for internal and external parties.

## Penyampaian Laporan Pelanggaran

Mekanisme pelaporan WBS kepada Perseroan melalui:

- a. Surat  dengan  tujuan  Corporate  Secretary  Perseroan. Pelapor  wajib  menuliskan  'RAHASIA'  pada  pojok  kiri atas amplop.
- b. Email ditujukan ke whistleblower@champ-group.com

## Perlindungan Pelapor

Pelapor  tidak  perlu  takut  atau  khawatir,  karena  Perseroan akan melindungi pelapor, meliputi jaminan kerahasiaan identitas  pelapor  dan  isi  laporan  serta  jaminan  keamanan pelapor terhadap perlakuan yang merugikan, seperti pemecatan yang tidak adil, penurunan jabatan atau pangkat, pelecehan  atau  diskriminasi  dalam  segala  bentuknya.  Jika hasil penyelidikan yang dilakukan oleh tim investigasi tidak  terbukti  adanya  pelanggaran,  pelapor  tidak  akan mendapatkan sanksi dari Perseroan.

## Penanganan Pengaduan

Dalam hal terdapat pengaduan, Perseroan akan melakukan investigasi  menyeluruh  terhadap  laporan  yang  diterima untuk segera ditindaklanjuti.

## Pihak Pengelola Pengaduan

Sistem WBS Perseroan dikelola oleh Corporate Secretary dan Unit Audit Internal.

## Jumlah Pengaduan

Sepanjang  tahun  2022,  Perseroan  tidak  menerima  adanya laporan  pengaduan  terhadap  pelanggaran.  Perseroan  juga tidak menerima laporan pelanggaran pada tahun 2021, dan oleh sebab itu tidak ada investigasi atau tindak lanjut yang dilakukan oleh Perseroan.

## KEBIJAKAN ANTIKORUPSI

Untuk menjalankan kegiatan usaha yang bersih dan profesional,  Perseroan  telah  membentuk  kebijakan  terkait dengan  antikorupsi.  Kebijakan  ini  berlaku  untuk  semua karyawan Perseroan di seluruh level jabatan tanpa terkecuali, dimana  Perseroan  berkomitmen  mendukung  pemerintah dalam mencegah dan memberantas korupsi.

2

0

## Submission of Violation Reports

Mechanism  of  submitting  the    violation  reports  shall  be conducted by:

- a. Submitting an official letter through a confidential e-mail addressed to the Corporate Secretary. The whistleblower shall write 'CONFIDENTIAL' in the upper left corner of the envelope.
- b. Sending an email to whistleblower@champ-group.com

## Protection to the Whistleblower

The whistleblower shall not be afraid or worried since the Company shall  protect  the  whistleblower  by  guaranteeing the confidentiality of the whistleblower's identity and contents  of  the  report  as  well  as  the  security  against adverse treatment and retaliation, including unfair dismissal, demotion, harassment or discrimination in any forms. In case the violation investigated by the investigation team is proven to be false or invalid, the whistleblower shall not receive any sanctions from the Company.

## Complaint Handling

The  Company  shall  conduct  a  thorough  investigation  on report of violations for immediately followed up.

## Party in Charge of Managing the Complaints

The Company's WBS system is managed by the Corporate Secretary and the Internal Audit Unit.

## Number of Complaints

Throughtout 2022, the Company did not receive any reports of violations. In addition, the Company also did not receive any reports of violations in 2021, and thus no investigations or follow-up were carried out by the Company.

## ANTI-CORRUPTION POLICY

In  an  effort  to  carry  out  reliable  and  professional  business activities,  the  Company  set  the  policies  related  to  anticorruption. These policies are applied to all employees at all levels of positions without any exception as the Company is committed to supporting the government in preventing and eradicating corruption.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Perseroan  mewajibkan  seluruh  Dewan  Komisaris,  Direksi, dan karyawannya untuk menjunjung tinggi persaingan usaha yang adil, kejujuran dan profesionalisme serta prinsip-prinsip GCG. Adapun tujuan diterapkannya kebijakan antikorupsi di Perseroan adalah untuk:

1. meningkatkan ketaatan dan kedisiplinan Perseroan terhadap hukum, peraturan dan etika serta menjalankan bisnis yang bersih dengan mendukung pemerintah dalam mencegah tindakan korupsi.
2. mencegah kerugian secara material maupun immaterial yang memengaruhi kegiatan Perseroan.

Kebijakan  ini  wajib  diaktualisasikan  oleh  seluruh  individu perusahaan. Secara berkala, Perseroan melakukan sosialisasi yang berkesinambungan kepada seluruh karyawan Perseroan melalui  berbagai  forum  pertemuan  dan  kegiatan  internal. Pada  tahun  2022,  Perseroan  mulai  mengadakan  pelatihan terkait antikorupsi.

## PENERAPAN ATAS PEDOMAN TATA KELOLA PERUSAHAAN TERBUKA

<!-- image -->

Besides, the Company ensures that the Board of Commissioners, the Board of Directors, and employees continue to carry out a fair, honest, and professional business competition, as well as implementing the GCG principles. The purpose of implementing anti-corruption policy in the Company is to:

1. improve the Company's compliance with laws, regulations, and ethics, including honesty in business by supporting the government in preventing corruption.
2. prevent material and immaterial losses that may affect the Company's operations.

This policy shall be implemented by all employees. On  a  regular  basis,  the  Company  carries  out  ongoing dissemination to employees through a forum meeting and internal activities. In 2022, the Company started to conduct training related to anti-corruption.

The  Board  of  Commissioners  has  a  self-  assessment policy to assess the performance.  Perseroan telah menerapkan kebijakan  self-assessment  dalam menilai kinerja Dewan Komisaris.  The Company has implemented a self-assessment policy  to assess the Board of Commissioners' performance.  Kebijakan  penilaian  sendiri  (  self-assessment  )  untuk  menilai  kinerja  Dewan  Komisaris  diungkapkan  melalui  Laporan  Tahunan  Terintegrasi Perseroan.  Self-assessment  policy  to  assess  Board  of  Commissioners performance is disclosed in the  Company's Integrated Annual Report. Dewan  Komisaris  telah  memiliki  kebijakan  terkait  pengunduran  diri  apabila  terlibat  dalam  kejahatan  keuangan, merujuk pada Pedoman Kerja serta Anggaran  Dasar Perseroan dan UU PT No. 40/2007.  The Board of Commissioners has a policy of  resignation  if  involved  in  financial  crimes,  in  accordance  with  the  Company's  Guidelines  and  the  Company's  Articles  of  Association as well as Law of Limited Liability Company  No. 40/2007.  Dewan Komisaris atau komite yang menjalankan  fungsi  nominasi  dan  remunerasi  menyusun  kebijakan  suksesi  dalam  proses  nominasi  anggota Direksi. Penyusunan kebijakan suksesi  dan proses nominasi Direksi tersebut belum dilakukan  dan  akan  dilakukan  dalam  rangka  penyempurnaan  penerapan GCG di Perseroan.  Up to this report issue, the Board of Commissioners has  carried out the nomination and remuneration functions  to formulate succession policy and nomination process  in  compliance  with  the  applicable  regulations.  The  preparation of succession policy and nomination process  for the Board of Directors has not yet completed and  going forward, it will be completed to improve the GCG  implementation within the Company.   |

Penentuan komposisi anggota Direksi Perseroan telah  memperhatikan  aspek  keberagaman  dan  informasi  tersebut  ditampilkan  dalam  bab  Profil  Direksi  dalam  Laporan Tahunan terintegrasi Perseroan ini halaman 58-  60.  Determination  of  the  composition  of  the  members  of  the Board of Directors takes into account the diversity  of  required  skills,  knowledge,  and  experience.  This  particular  information  can  be  found  in  the  Profile  Section  of  the  Board  of  Directors  in  the  Company's  integrated Annual Report on page 58-60.  Anggota  Direksi  yang  membawahi  bidang  akuntansi  atau  keuangan  memiliki  keahlian  dan/atau pengetahuan di bidang akuntansi.  The Director who is responsible for accounting  or  finance  has  relevant  expertise  and/or  knowledge in accounting.  Perseroan  memiliki  anggota  Direksi  yang  membawahi  bidang akuntansi dan keuangan serta memiliki keahlian  dan  pengetahuan  di  bidang  akuntansi.  Informasi  tersebut  ditampilkan  dalam  bab  Profil  Direksi  dalam  Laporan Tahunan terintegrasi Perseroan ini halaman 60.  The Company has a member of the Board of Directors  who  is  in  charge  of  accounting  and  finance  and  has  expertise and knowledge in accounting. ## Strategi Keberlanjutan

Sustainability Strategies

<!-- image -->

Untuk mempertahankan  bisnisnya  secara berkelanjutan, Perseroan merancang dan melaksanakan berbagai strategi keberlanjutan yang terklasifikasikan  dalam  aspek  ekonomi, sosial,  dan  lingkungan.  Penerapan  strategi  keberlanjutan ini  bertujuan  untuk  mendorong  terciptanya  pembangunan berkelanjutan, searah dengan Tujuan Pembangunan Berkelanjutan (TPB).

TPB merupakan sebuah rencana aksi global yang digagas dan disepakati  oleh  para  pemimpin  dunia,  termasuk  Indonesia. TPB mencakup 17 tujuan dan 169 target yang diprioritaskan untuk mengatasi isu-isu global besar, dan diharapkan dapat  memenuhi  cakupan  TPB.  Untuk  itu,  Perseroan  juga mengarahkan strategi keberlanjutannya dengan berdasarkan pada aspek-aspek TPB.

Dalam aspek ekonomi, Perseroan terus berupaya mengembangkan  produk-produk baru, mengoptimalkan layanan kepada pelanggan serta memastikan terlaksananya sistem produksi dan logistik yang berkelanjutan dan bertanggung-jawab.  Dalam  aspek  lingkungan,  Perseroan senantiasa mengupayakan efisiensi dalam penggunaan energi,  air,  dan  bahan  bakar  baik  di  kantor  pusat  dan  di seluruh  restoran.  Selain  itu,  dalam  aspek  sosial,  Perseroan turut  bekerjasama  dan  berkolaborasi  dengan  pihak-pihak ketiga untuk senantiasa memberikan dukungan dan bantuan bagi masyarakat dan komunitas yang memerlukan.

To sustain its business, the Company develops and implements various sustainability strategies, classified into economic,  social,  and  environmental  aspects.  This sustainability  strategy  aims  to  encourage  the  creation  of sustainable development in accordance with the Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).

SDGs are a global  action  plan  developed  and  adopted  by international  leaders,  including  leaders  in  Indonesia.  The Company's  SDGs  consist  of  17  goals  and  169  targets  that have been prioritized to address major global issues and are expected to fulfill the scope of the SDGs. The Company also directs its sustainability strategy based on these SDG factors for these purposes.

In the economic aspect, the Company continues to develop new products, optimize service to customers, and ensure the implementation of a sustainable and responsible production and  logistics  system.  In  the  environmental  aspect,  the Company continuously strives for efficiency when utilizing energy,  water,  and  fuel  at  the  head  office  and  in  all  of  its restaurants.  Furthermore,  in  terms  of  social  responsibility, the  Company  also  cooperates  and  collaborates  with  third parties  to  provide  for  and  improve  the  quality  of  life  for underprivileged people and communities.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## Perubahan Signifikan pada Perusahaan
